# The unicorn board (LICORNE, loop mode 11) of the original Game.exe (GOG.com, md5 eb06eaa718877dfcabee6e8f89d85b31)
# with spr_p6.dll under emulation, 800 x 600 RGB565, the Pentium II routines (x87 0x0e7f), started at 100 s; frame n
# runs at 100 + n * 1.0025 / 15 s. Before the runs: cells (the 2D cell positions per row), maze (the openings N W S
# E per cell), dropmap (the drop cell of a carried beacon, pointer every 10 px). Per run: pointer / click / rclick
# (the input written before frame f), snd (load, play, stop, test -> playing), cmd (RunOneCommand), draw (the
# warp, black here; the sprite calls: BANK#F@X,Y*ALPHA, C: a centred call), poly <f> <group> <n> <md5> (the
# polygons given to the rasteriser per object: uni the unicorn, tri the beast, bal the beacons, held the carried
# beacon redrawn; md5 of their 0x24-byte vertex records), md5 (the screen at the flip), path (the unicorn's node
# list: x,y,direction,beacon), ret (the frame returned 0: the stop ran), scan (CtrlScenarIN), st (the board's
# globals that changed; float bits for uf tf um tm rt). The game's log lines are left out.
